# Claim ledger: markets levels 1 to 3, round 3 (audit-markets-1-3-r3)

Blind read of commit 0bf8e2e2. Stage 1 course, so the full ledger: one row for each prose, question, key, slip, option, hint, feedback line, explain sentence, summary line, model paragraph and rubric line. Rows were generated from the JSON after the read (scratch script `ledger.py`), with the verdict and basis set by the auditor; every row marked "recomputed" is a check in `docs/reports/markets-L1-3-concepts-r3.py`. Hull section numbers are the 10th edition.

Rows: 3221. Verdicts: correct 3216, imprecise 5, wrong 0, misleading 0, unverified 0.
